# Juno's sounds: <sound id> = <file under E:/Overwatch Archive/extracted/154088/> (build 154088). Same format as
# brigitte.txt / sombra.txt; build_asset_pack.py sounds copies / mixes them into the local pack. Nothing here has been
# listened to. Read 2026-11-01 offline (Overwatch and Battle.net checked closed before every archive read and DataTool /
# TankLib run): her 02C sounds' Wwise banks decoded exactly (ww.py, dump_banks.py, emote_events.py / wwfull.py from
# research/emote-sounds/scripts: children from each object's parent id, random / sequence / switch / layer parameters,
# volume, make-up gain, pitch, delays, the events' actions, RTPC curves), the Init bank's bus volumes and game parameter
# defaults (1E818, stmg.py). Scripts: E:/Overwatch Archive/research/juno/scripts/sounds/ (juno_gen.py writes this file;
# Brigitte's pipeline copied and retargeted); their data (decode juno_ww.json, bank dump banks_raw.json, measurements
# juno_measure.json, her projectiles' and child entities' effects fx_proj.json, contact sets contacts.json, voice lines
# voice_lines.json, the RTPC defaults rtpc_defaults.json): research/juno/sounds/. Effect and clip timings:
# research/juno/work/fx_sounds_timed.json (fx_sounds2_juno.py over work/graph_effects.json) and work/clip_sounds.txt;
# graph nodes: research/juno/work_kit decompiles (fx_nodes.py: work/fx_nodes_out.txt) and kit_juno.md; the kit itself:
# tools/reference/juno-map.md; this map read for wiring: tools/reference/juno-sounds.md.
# The live kit: Mediblaster, Pulsar Torpedoes, Hyper Ring, Orbital Ray, Martian Overboots (double jump, hover), Glide Boost;
# her live perks Locked On and the ring's refund as EXTRA, jump / land / quick melee foley as EXTRA. The Stadium powers and
# copies (5540, 65C3, the charge effects) are left out ('Not mapped').
# Files: her skin and voice extraction are in research/juno/extract, not under the build root, so those paths reach it
# through ../../research/juno/extract/; the 28 banks it lacked (contact sets and projectile slots) were extracted for this
# map into research/juno/sounds/ExtraSounds/Juno (extract_banks.py, DataTool's own sound export; manifest.json there:
# MediblasterContact, TorpedoContact, Projectiles), reached through ../../research/juno/sounds/ExtraSounds/Juno/. Ids: 02C
# sounds (B3DC = 00000000B3DC.02C), 00D effects, 006 clips, 08F clip animation effects, 049 contact sets; graphs 01B with
# their node numbers. Confidence: H read in the data, M inferred, L guess.
# Gains: Wwise volume + make-up gain summed from each file up through its containers and mixers (the middle of a random
# range included) plus its output bus chain, plus RTPC curves (dB scaling 20*log10(1+y); linear between points) and
# SetVolume actions. RTPCs are read at their game parameter's default (the Init bank's list); modulators: an LFO at its
# middle, a time modulator at its start on one-shots and at its end on loops (the Mediblaster volley's at its start). As
# in brigitte.txt, ONE offset per family keeps her sounds' balance with each other, bringing the family's loudest single
# layer to -1 dBFS (the builder's limiter catches sums): own +0.5 dB (loudest: juno.mediblaster.volley), 3p +1 dB
#  (loudest: juno.ult.cast_3p).
# Pitch: cents as a speed factor (*). Low / high-pass filters, random pitch / volume per play, fades, Seek, Wwise states
# and the voice priority RTPCs are not reproduced. Layers more than 60 dB down are left out, and in loops those 20 dB
# under the loudest loop. A loop's LOOP LENGTH is its longest bed within 6 dB of the loudest layer; shorter layers are
# tiled across it and beds more than 15 % longer are left out. Self-repeating streams (trigger-rate containers: the
# Mediblaster's 5C2D, the lock-on tick 5C17) are built as their period's repeats across a fixed window (the volley's
# 0.353 s; 1.0 s of the tick), from the period at the fire-rate parameter's default.
# Own vs others: her STUSounds with audience 1 (B3DC, B10F, AF2A, B8CE, B2CF) are read as hers (H by Lucio's pairs); the
# other no-range copies (audience 2) are read as the player the effect plays on (M, Ana's / Sombra's / Brigitte's reading):
# 'own' is Juno, or the healed / boosted / amplified ally where a rule says. Ranged copies are everyone else's ('3p');
# where an effect or contact holds two ranged copies with the same files they are read as the two teams' (or near / far)
# copies (L which): the louder is built, the other noted; copies with different files all play (built together, M).
# Effect sounds with no own copy (the ring, the ray, the torpedoes in flight, contact hits on the world) are built once
# for everyone ('both'). Ranges come through the volume (sounds.md).

# ===== How each game event is put together (rules)
# juno.mediblaster.volley: Mediblaster volley, own: 5C2D's B3DC (no range, audience 1): its first-shot containers (a
#  random 15 of 0.06-0.08 s, a random 2 of 0.10 s at +300 cents) once and its stream containers (0.06-0.17 s sets
#  every 0.06 s, a 0.06-0.07 s set every 0.10 s) repeated across the 0.353 s volley. Time modulators read at their
#  start.
# Trigger: each volley (PRIMARY held: 4445 #913 StateWeaponVolley, 12 projectiles at 34 / s = 0.353 s, one volley per
#  0.80 s) holds #1114 StateStack -> #1211 effect 5C2D on her (H) for the volley; 5C2D's 02C are self-repeating
#  streams (trigger-rate random containers paced by the shared fire-rate parameter 4066808683, as Sombra's pistol:
#  0.06 s at its default 100, H; M that her game leaves it there), so one Play sounds the whole volley until the Stop
#  as it ends.
# Layers: a B3DC 418551166 RanSeq 0.06-0.07 s x15, net -3.5, played x4; b B3DC 547810568 RanSeq 0.06-0.08 s x15, net
#  -2.5; c B3DC 468215347 RanSeq 0.14-0.17 s x15 +150 cents, net -4.5, played x6; d B3DC 1050224370 RanSeq 0.07-0.08 s
#  x10 +150 cents, net -10.5, played x6; e B3DC 107164974 RanSeq 0.11 s x6 +684 cents, net -20, played x6; f B3DC
#  363201613 RanSeq 0.10 s x2 +300 cents, net -5.5; g B3DC 510093861 RanSeq 0.63 s -150 cents, net -30, played x4.
# Left out: B3DC 54340537 (-121 dB).
@juno.mediblaster.volley = juno.mediblaster.volley.a:-3 + juno.mediblaster.volley.a@0.1:-3 + juno.mediblaster.volley.a@0.2:-3 + juno.mediblaster.volley.a@0.3:-3 + juno.mediblaster.volley.b:-2 + juno.mediblaster.volley.c*1.091@0.04:-4 + juno.mediblaster.volley.c*1.091@0.1:-4 + juno.mediblaster.volley.c*1.091@0.16:-4 + juno.mediblaster.volley.c*1.091@0.22:-4 + juno.mediblaster.volley.c*1.091@0.28:-4 + juno.mediblaster.volley.c*1.091@0.34:-4 + juno.mediblaster.volley.d*1.091@0.04:-10 + juno.mediblaster.volley.d*1.091@0.1:-10 + juno.mediblaster.volley.d*1.091@0.16:-10 + juno.mediblaster.volley.d*1.091@0.22:-10 + juno.mediblaster.volley.d*1.091@0.28:-10 + juno.mediblaster.volley.d*1.091@0.34:-10 + juno.mediblaster.volley.e*1.485@0.03:-19.5 + juno.mediblaster.volley.e*1.485@0.09:-19.5 + juno.mediblaster.volley.e*1.485@0.15:-19.5 + juno.mediblaster.volley.e*1.485@0.21:-19.5 + juno.mediblaster.volley.e*1.485@0.27:-19.5 + juno.mediblaster.volley.e*1.485@0.33:-19.5 + juno.mediblaster.volley.f*1.189@0.03:-5 + juno.mediblaster.volley.g*0.917:-29.5 + juno.mediblaster.volley.g*0.917@0.1:-29.5 + juno.mediblaster.volley.g*0.917@0.2:-29.5 + juno.mediblaster.volley.g*0.917@0.3:-29.5
# juno.mediblaster.volley_end: Mediblaster volley tail, own: B3DC's random 11 of 2.08-2.72 s ring-outs (+302 cents; in
#  the game one per repeat, layered) and its Stop-event set (a random 8 of 0.37-0.41 s, very quiet).
# Trigger: the volley ends (#913 ends, 5C2D stops: B3DC's Stop event plays its tail set, H) at 0.353 s; the stream's
#  long ring-out container is built here once.
# Layers: a B3DC 415586142 RanSeq 2.08-2.72 s x11 +828 cents, net -15.5; b B3DC Stop-event 948263431 RanSeq 0.37-0.41
#  s x8 +300 cents, net -43.5.
@juno.mediblaster.volley_end = juno.mediblaster.volley_end.a*1.613@0.03:-15 + juno.mediblaster.volley_end.b*1.189:-43

# numbers. Orbital Ray's press raises 184D (own, key F1AC), 16A9 (allies, F1AB) and 16AA (enemies, F1AA) together (4A44 #414
